> help
One action = one game-hour; queries take no time. Sleep keeps you in bed at least 4 hours (8 when you camp); wait to sleep on.
Eat defaults to ONE serving; use eat hardtack 2 for two. Need effects use the previous hour's activity.
Out on open ground with bedding, sleep makes camp where you stand; wait follows your lodging order, returning to a nearby paid or own bed when available.

buy <item> [qty] | sell <item> [qty] | eat <item> [servings] [method] | cook <item> [servings] [method]
drink | meal | sleep | rest | work | wait <Nh|Nd> | wait until <HH:00|dawn|morning|noon|dusk|night|tomorrow>
prices | places [page] | standing <sleep|eat|drink|warmth|lodging> <on|off> | agency <commanded|psyche>
inspect <person/item/place> | pack <item> into <container> (moves one) | unpack <item>
aid <person> | retire | depart | rations <10..150> | plan | journal (lists what you owe) | explain <event id>
Enter a menu number or stable action id. help

> help
One action = one game-hour; queries take no time. Sleep keeps you in bed at least 4 hours (8 when you camp); wait to sleep on.
Eat defaults to ONE serving; use eat hardtack 2 for two. Need effects use the previous hour's activity.
Out on open ground with bedding, sleep makes camp where you stand; wait follows your lodging order, returning to a nearby paid or own bed when available.
look | signs | investigate <sign id> | actions [page] | go <place name/id> | talk <person> | <interaction> <person>
buy <item> [qty] | sell <item> [qty] | eat <item> [servings] [method] | cook <item> [servings] [method]
drink | meal | sleep | rest | work | wait <Nh|Nd> | wait until <HH:00|dawn|morning|noon|dusk|night|tomorrow>
prices | places [page] | standing <sleep|eat|drink|warmth|lodging> <on|off> | agency <commanded|psyche>
inspect <person/item/place> | pack <item> into <container> (moves one) | unpack <item>
aid <person> | retire | depart | rations <10..150> | plan | journal (lists what you owe) | explain <event id>
Enter a menu number or stable action id. help

> journal
1855-04-01 00:00 Sunday, the 1st of April, 1855. The Sabbath under a full moon; the evening service will want no lantern. Rain in the evening.
1855-04-01 05:00 At Dunleavy's Tavern: The broom leans bristles upward against the shut door. I would like to know what made it.
1855-04-01 05:00 A report from Dunleavy's Tavern: The broom leans bristles upward against the shut door.
1855-04-01 06:00 Lydia Pratt helped Gussie Pratt at Pratt & Daughter, Outfitters. I saw it, and thought the better of her for it.
1855-04-01 17:00 Lydia Pratt comforted Gussie Pratt at Pratt & Daughter, Outfitters. I saw it, and thought the better of her for it.
1855-04-01 23:00 I shut a door in myself today. I cannot say which, only that the house is quieter.
1855-04-02 00:00 Monday, the 2nd of April, 1855. Full moon, and bright enough to read a letter by.
1855-04-02 09:00 Lydia Pratt made a gift to Gussie Pratt at Pratt & Daughter, Outfitters. I saw it, and thought the better of her for it.
1855-04-02 12:00 Lydia Pratt lent money to Gussie Pratt at Pratt & Daughter, Outfitters. Money passes, and so do obligations; I have begun to keep a list.
1855-04-02 16:00 Monday. Gussie Pratt comforted me, unasked. There are people in this county who keep the word neighbor alive.
1855-04-02 18:00 I notice I am saying the same thing to myself over and over, in the same words. A person ought to be able to say a thing once and be done.
1855-04-02 18:00 It was the sensible thing; anyone would agree. I have asked myself and I agree.
1855-04-03 00:00 Tuesday, the 3rd of April, 1855. The full moon. The bluffs stand out white and the dogs are restless.
1855-04-03 00:00 I put this down before memory alters it. Pratt & Daughter, Outfitters: A neat plait has appeared in the loose straw by the door.
1855-04-03 02:00 I have a great many reasons this week, and I do not trust any of them. I would rather have one true thing.
1855-04-03 02:00 There is nothing in it. I looked at it from three sides and there is nothing in it. I shall not think of it again.
1855-04-03 08:00 Nora Dunleavy was in close talk with Lucius Vance at Dunleavy's Tavern on Tuesday in the forenoon. I would give a good deal to know the subject.
1855-04-04 00:00 Wednesday, the 4th of April, 1855; the waning gibbous. Snow in the morning.
1855-04-04 00:00 I went to bed parched. A person can learn a good deal about the world from the floor of an empty belly.
1855-04-04 05:00 Michael Dunleavy helped Amos Whitcomb at Dunleavy's Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of him for it.
1855-04-04 10:00 Lucius Vance helped Michael Dunleavy at Dunleavy's Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of him for it.
1855-04-04 13:00 Gussie Pratt was in close talk with Lydia Pratt at Pratt & Daughter, Outfitters on Wednesday in the afternoon. I would give a good deal to know the subject.
1855-04-05 00:00 Thursday, the 5th of April, 1855. The waning gibbous.
1855-04-05 00:00 I am down with want of food. Too long on salt meat and meal; the body keeps its own books.
Since last look: no time passed.

Goat prairie, steep to the southwest, bluestem and pasqueflower between the ledges. Survey stakes run across it. Spring at Huldra's Shelf. The wind carries the smell of wet earth. Amos Whitcomb, Joel Pike are here. A cow-bell sounds somewhere beyond the empty ledges. There is danger here; the ordinary rules feel uncertain. The iron-shod survey post stands silent; the bells keep away.
Open sign: The broom leans bristles upward against the shut door. (investigate 4:0:dawn)
Open sign: A neat plait has appeared in the loose straw by the door. (investigate 33:2:midnight)
